**LDP \-- LDP IPv6配置命令 \-- ipv6 import bgp**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[ipv6 import bgp]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x925498374}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[引入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[单播路由。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo ipv6 import bgp]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x803035595}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_189675388}

[**[ipv6 import bgp]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x812844429}

[**[undo ipv6 import bgp]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1219451505}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1498456407}

[[LDP ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1390983365}[不主动引入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[单播路由。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1825830400}

[[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1753952984}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/LDP-VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x141346117}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x2076945076}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_211295378}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1391048901}

[[缺省情况下，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_300419593}[自动引入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由（包括已引入到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[单播路由），并为通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSP]{lang="EN-US"}[触发策略的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由和通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSP]{lang="EN-US"}[触发策略的带标签]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP]{lang="EN-US"}[路由分配标签，但不自动引入未被引入到]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[单播路由。这就导致了在一些特殊的组网环境下，如在运营商的运营商组网中，如果一级运营商的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[PE]{lang="EN-US"}[与二级运营商]{style="font-family:宋体"}[CE]{lang="EN-US"}[之间未配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[OSPF]{lang="EN-US"}[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IS-IS]{lang="EN-US"}[等]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IGP]{lang="EN-US"}[协议，则无法通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[单播路由分配标签，因而无法建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP LSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。有关运营商的运营商组网的详细信息，请参见"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS]{lang="EN-US"}[配置指导"中的"]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}["。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[通过配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1540645459}[引入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[单播路由，可将]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[单播路由强制引入至]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[，如果该路由通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSP]{lang="EN-US"}[触发策略，则为其分配标签建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[配置此命令后，可能会导致]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1958550081}[引入大量的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[单播路由，占用设备的标签、内存资源等问题，建议仅在必要时使用此命令。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x589075103}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_2126220044}[配置公网]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[实例中，强制引入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[BGP IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[单播路由，触发建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP LSP]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1390590149}

[\[Sysname\] mpls ldp]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-ldp\] ipv6 import bgp]{lang="EN-US"}

[[【相关命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x79006512}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[ipv6 lsp-trigger]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_97408_54739_299008787}
:::

**LDP \-- LDP IPv6配置命令 \-- mpls ldp ipv6 enable**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls ldp ipv6]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **enable**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1172779443}[命令用来使能接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls ldp ipv6 ]{lang="EN-US"}[enable]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_97408_54739_334079919}[命令用来关闭接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x356428573}

[**[mpls ldp ipv6]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **enable**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x195127450}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1894977960}

[[未使能接口的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x712520669}[支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:
黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_2216579}

[[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1803803321}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x328685502}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x2113756215}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1390721221}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_647641491}

[[如果在接口上使能了]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x731649685}[能力和]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[能力，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[且该接口处于]{style="font-family:宋体"}[up]{lang="EN-US"}[状态，则]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[会在该接口上启动基本发现机制，通过该接口发送]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Link hello]{lang="EN-US"}[消息，]{style="font-family:宋体"}[hello]{lang="EN-US"}[消息源地址以及携带的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TCP]{lang="EN-US"}[传输地址的地址类型为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[需要注意的是：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x2141121767}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果接口上仅使能了]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1973363221}[支持]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[能力，则必须使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls ldp transport-address]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令配置]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[传输地址后，才会发送]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 hello]{lang="EN-US"}[消息。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[执行本命令前，需要先在系统视图下执行]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x703682105}**[mpls ldp]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令全局使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LSR]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[如果接口已与某个]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_678955117}[实例绑定，则需要通过]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[vpn-instance]{lang="EN-US"}**[命令使能]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[此]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例的]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[同一接口上]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1390786757}[既]{style="font-family:宋体"}[可以通过配置]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls ldp enable]{lang="EN-US"}**[使能]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[v4]{lang="EN-US"}[能力，也可以通过配置]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[mpls ldp ipv6 enable]{lang="EN-US"}**[使能]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[v6]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x892412110}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[路由应用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_963198190}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1490370704}[在接口]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GigabitEthernet1/0/1]{lang="EN-US"}[上使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[支持]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[能力。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**LDP \-- LDP IPv6配置命令 \-- mpls ldp transport-address**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[mpls ldp transport-address]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1466040554}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[传输地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo mpls ldp transport-address]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x125878649}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1775019696}

[[接口视图下：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1351214196}

[**[mpls ldp transport-address]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ipv6-address*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1390852294}

[**[undo mpls ldp transport-address ]{lang="EN-US"}***[ipv6]{lang="EN-US"}[-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x620198329}

[[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1038853550}[对等体视图下：]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[mpls ldp transport-address]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ipv6-address* ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1874823345}

[**[undo mpls ldp transport-address]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1328654494}

[[未配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1390917830}[传输地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1447089085}

[[接口视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/LDP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1041998527}[对等体视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1980089753}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1581008696}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1558565522}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1927601512}

[*[ipv6-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1390983366}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[使用此地址作为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[传输地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_259746459}

[[LSR]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x658039022}[在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Link hello]{lang="EN-US"}[消息或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Targeted hello]{lang="EN-US"}[消息中携带传输地址，将本端的传输地址通知给对端。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1203166749}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1894108470}[配置与对等体建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[TCP]{lang="EN-US"}[连接时采用的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[传输地址为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2002]{lang="EN-US"}[：：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> System-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_457225145}

[\[Sysname\] mpls ldp]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-ldp\] targeted-peer 2001::1 ]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-ldp-peer-2001::1\] mpls ldp transport-address 2002::1]{lang="EN-US"}

[]{#_Toc404790693}[]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1408754369}[]{#_Toc383443268}

**LDP \-- LDP IPv6配置命令 \-- targeted-peer**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[targeted-peer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_97408_54739_753394886}[命令用来配置主动向指定对等体的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址发送]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Targeted hello]{lang="EN-US"}[消息来建立]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[会话，允许应答指定对等体的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Targeted hello]{lang="EN-US"}[消息，并进入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[对等体视图。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo targeted-peer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x134897321}[命令用来取消向指定对等体的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址发送]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Targeted hello]{lang="EN-US"}[消息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1157135032}

[**[targeted-peer]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ipv6-address*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1390590150}

[**[undo targeted-peer]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ipv6-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x75623524}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x27612453}

[[设备不会主动向对等体发送]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Targeted hello]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1707267175}[消息，也不会应答对等体的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Targeted hello]{lang="EN-US"}[消息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1937474047}

[[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_22954535}[视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1240339774}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1390655686}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x833905395}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1456890886}

[*[ipv6-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1504119475}[：指定对等体的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1765425460}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[通过]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[targeted-peer]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ipv6-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_97408_54739_809015884}[命令指定]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[ IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[对等体，并进入对等体视图后，必须在该视图下配置]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[传输地址，]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[LDP]{lang="EN-US"}[才会发送]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 Targeted hello]{lang="EN-US"}[消息。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[若要保证建立]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 Targeted hello]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1390721222}[邻接关系，需]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:
宋体"}[保证]{style="font-family:宋体"}[对等体两端配置的一致性，即在本端使用]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}**[targeted-peer]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ipv6-address]{lang="EN-US"}*[命令配置的目的地址需为对端在对等体视图下配置的传输地址，并保证本端传输地址与目的地址之间路由可达。]{lang="EN-US" style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_1050926018}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_995584073}[配置向对等体的指定地址]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2001::1]{lang="EN-US"}[发送]{style="font-family:宋体"}[Targeted hello]{lang="EN-US"}[消息，并进入]{style="font-family:宋体"}[L]{lang="EN-US"}[DP]{lang="EN-US"}[对等体视图*。*]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[\<]{lang="EN-US"}*[Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_97408_54739_601965474}

[\[Sysname\] mpls ldp]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-ldp\] targeted-peer 2001::1]{lang="EN-US"}

[\[Sysname-ldp-peer-2001::1\]]{lang="EN-US"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}**[display mpls ldp peer]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_97408_54739_x1390786758}
